shared_focus = {
	id = AXIS_joint_alliance
	
	dynamic = yes 
	
	icon = GFX_focus_generic_fascist_propaganda
	

	x = 30
	y = 0


	allow_branch = {
		has_country_flag = shared_tree_clicked
	}
	

	allow_branch = {NOT = {tag = D01}}

	cost = 0

	text_icon = NORDIC_focus_style

	available = {
		is_in_faction_with = GER
		is_subject = no
		is_ai = no
	}
    bypass = 
    {
        is_in_faction_with = GER
		is_ai = no
    }

	bypass_effect = {
		add_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = axis_dynamic }
	}

	ai_will_do = {
		base = 1
		modifier = {
			factor = 100
			is_historical_focus_on = yes 
		}
	}

	search_filters = { FOCUS_FILTER_POLITICAL }

	completion_reward = {
	    if = {
	        limit = { is_in_faction_with = GER }

		
		    }
	}
}

joint_focus = {
	id = AXIS_political_cooperation
	icon = GFX_focus_generic_improve_the_administration
	prerequisite = { focus = AXIS_joint_alliance }

	x = 0
	y = 1
	relative_position_id = AXIS_joint_alliance
	cost = 5

	text_icon = NORDIC_focus_style

	available = {
		is_in_faction_with = GER
		
	}

	search_filters = { FOCUS_FILTER_POLITICAL }

	completion_reward_joint_originator = {
    every_country = {
        limit = {
            OR = {
                tag = GER
				tag = ITA
                tag = ROM
				tag = SPR
				tag = HUN
                tag = BUL
            }
        }
        add_political_power = 25
        add_stability = 0.1
        add_popularity = {
            ideology = fascism
            popularity = 0.1
        }
    }
}

}

	    		tooltip = production_factory_efficiency_gain_factor_tt 
	    	}

joint_focus = {
	id = AXIS_romania_navy_docks
	icon = GFX_goal_generic_construct_naval_dockyard
	prerequisite = { focus = AXIS_romanian_specialization }

	x = -1
	y = 1
	relative_position_id = AXIS_romanian_specialization
	cost = 5

	text_icon = NORDIC_focus_style

	available = {
		is_in_faction_with = GER
	}

	allow_branch = 
	{
		ROM = {has_country_flag = navy_specialization_flag}
	}

	mutually_exclusive = {focus = AXIS_romania_navy_output}

	search_filters = { FOCUS_FILTER_POLITICAL }

	completion_reward_joint_originator = 
	{
	    if = {
	        limit = { is_in_faction_with = GER }

				ROM = 
				{
						every_core_state = {
							limit = {
								is_fully_controlled_by = ROOT
								is_coastal = yes
							}
							random_select_amount = 2
							add_extra_state_shared_building_slots = 2
							add_building_construction = {
								type = dockyard
								level = 2
								instant_build = yes
							}
						}
							
				}
		    }
	}
}

	icon = GFX_goal_generic_construct_naval_dockyard
	prerequisite = { focus = AXIS_hungarian_specialization }

	x = -1
	y = 1
	relative_position_id = AXIS_hungarian_specialization
	cost = 5

	text_icon = NORDIC_focus_style

	available = {
		is_in_faction_with = GER
	}

	allow_branch = 
	{
		HUN = {has_country_flag = navy_specialization_flag}
	}

	mutually_exclusive = {focus = AXIS_hungary_navy_output}

	search_filters = { FOCUS_FILTER_POLITICAL }

	completion_reward_joint_originator = 
	{
	    if = {
	        limit = { is_in_faction_with = GER }

				HUN = 
				{
					every_core_state = {
						limit = {
							is_fully_controlled_by = ROOT
							is_coastal = yes
						}
						random_select_amount = 2
						add_extra_state_shared_building_slots = 2
						add_building_construction = {
							type = dockyard
							level = 2
							instant_build = yes
						}
					}
				}
		    }
	}
}

	id = AXIS_spain_navy_docks
	icon = GFX_goal_generic_construct_naval_dockyard
	prerequisite = { focus = AXIS_spanish_specialization }

	x = -1
	y = 1
	relative_position_id = AXIS_spanish_specialization
	cost = 5

	text_icon = NORDIC_focus_style

	available = {
		is_in_faction_with = GER
	}

	allow_branch = 
	{
		SPR = {has_country_flag = navy_specialization_flag}
	}

	mutually_exclusive = {focus = AXIS_spain_navy_output}

	search_filters = { FOCUS_FILTER_POLITICAL }

	completion_reward_joint_originator = 
	{
	    if = {
	        limit = { is_in_faction_with = GER }

				SPR = 
				{
						every_core_state = {
							limit = {
								is_fully_controlled_by = ROOT
								is_coastal = yes
							}
							random_select_amount = 2
							add_extra_state_shared_building_slots = 2
							add_building_construction = {
								type = dockyard
								level = 2
								instant_build = yes
							}
						}
							
				}
		    }
	}
}

	}

	        limit = { is_in_faction_with = GER }

				}
		    }
	}
